- Teacher scope: `GET /lesson-cancellations` requires `groupId`; teacher role is additionally constrained to groups they teach (returns `[]` for unauthorised groups instead of leaking 403/404).

#### Initial Balance (`POST /students/:id/initial-balance`)

CEO-only one-shot for centers transitioning to the new finance system. Writes a single `INITIAL_BALANCE` Transaction; the partial unique index enforces "at most one per student". P2002 is translated to `BadRequestException("Boshlang'ich balans bu o'quvchi uchun allaqachon kiritilgan")`.

#### Balance Withdrawal (`src/withdrawals/`)

Admin-driven drain of a student's positive balance into the centre's account — distinct from `Refunds`, which return money to the student. The amount is **revenue of the month it is withdrawn in** (ADR-0055): the canonical net profit adds it as its own leg, `NetProfit.balanceWithdrawals`, read by `loadBalanceWithdrawals` (`src/reports/balance-withdrawals.ts`, by `createdAt`, branch-scoped). It is never folded into `revenue`, which stays the lesson value that the month-end expectation, the collection ratio and the «Foyda tarkibi» forecast read; on the cash basis it is not added at all (the money counted as «Tushum» when it was paid). Used during onboarding/transition, when a student paid in advance and the rest of the balance must be recognised rather than left "muallaq" (floating), and from the «Qaytariladigan pul» page (`/payments/refunds`, «Markaz hisobiga o'tkazish»).

- **The month is not chosen.** The server books every withdrawal in the current Tashkent month: one `now` drives `Transaction.createdAt`, `metadata.targetMonth` and the teacher accrual's `lessonDate` (the withdrawal day, so it always lands in the open payroll period for any `cycleStartDay`). `CreateWithdrawalDto.targetMonth` is optional and deprecated; any other month is a 400 «Yechib olish faqat joriy oy uchun yoziladi». A past month would have changed that month's reported profit after the fact and parked the teacher's share in a closed payroll period, where the cron never pays it.
- **Endpoints**: `GET /withdrawals/preview/:studentId`, `POST /withdrawals`
- **Transfer condition (ADR-0077)**: `create` refuses (400) unless the student's latest **valid** balance notice (`BalanceNotice`, `createdAt ≥ Student.statusChangedAt`) exists and today (Tashkent) ≥ notice + 10 bank days + 30 days (`transferTerm`, `balance-notices/transfer-condition.ts`). The refusal texts are the spec's; `loadTransferState` is the one reader (create, preview's `transfer`, the refundable drawer). Every withdrawal, the profile's «Yechib olish» included. `allowedFrom` is never stored: it is recomputed on every read from the notice day and today's holiday rows, so a holiday switched off after a notice can open the gate earlier than the date the bot text promised. Notices: `POST /students/:id/balance-notices` (`balance.withdraw`, the withdrawal's own capability: CEO/BD/Admin) — `BOT` sends the CEO-approved text (`balance-notice-text.ts`, pinned by a test) at once through `SmsService` (an addition to ADR-0025's instant list) and writes the notice only when it was delivered (a failed send answers the fixed 400 «Botga xabar yetmadi — qo'ng'iroq qiling»); `CALL` marks a phone call.
- **Roles**: `CEO, Branch Director, Administrator` (class-level `@Roles`)
- **Transaction type**: `BALANCE_WITHDRAWAL` — reduces student balance, metadata stores `{ targetMonth, creditTeacher, teacherUserId, groupId, reason }` for audit
- **`creditTeacher` flag**: when true, also writes a `SalaryAccrual` linked via `deductionTransactionId` to the new BALANCE_WITHDRAWAL row. The accrual has `attendanceId IS NULL` (no underlying lesson) and `lessonDate` = the withdrawal day (Tashkent). The teacher must be on one of the student's active enrollments — service validates this via a `groupTeachers` join and throws `ForbiddenException` otherwise.
- **`SalaryAccrual` schema relax**: `attendanceId` is nullable; the previous unique constraint `(userId, studentId, groupId, lessonDate)` is replaced with `(userId, studentId, groupId, lessonDate, attendanceId)` so withdrawal accruals (NULL attendanceId) can stack within a month — Postgres treats NULLs as distinct in UNIQUE.
- **Atomicity**: balance check + transaction write + student balance update + optional accrual + EntityHistory record run inside one `Serializable` `prisma.$transaction` (10s maxWait, 15s timeout).
- **`To'lovlar` tab**: `BALANCE_WITHDRAWAL` is a money-flow type — included in the comma-separated `?types=` filter. That list is the tab's contract: **every type that moves the balance belongs in it** (`PAYMENT,REFUND,ADJUSTMENT,INITIAL_BALANCE,BALANCE_WITHDRAWAL,LESSON_DEDUCTION,DISCOUNT_ADJUSTMENT,DEBT_WRITE_OFF,MOCK_EXAM_FEE`) — a missing type shows the balance jumping with no visible cause, which is what hid 37 rows worth 4 296 450 so'm. Adding a new balance-moving `TransactionType` means adding it here AND to `TRANSACTION_TYPE_INFO` on the client. The `Lesson Trail` endpoint continues to scope strictly to `LESSON_DEDUCTION` + `LESSON_CONSUMPTION`.
- **Salary calculation**: existing `salary-summary` and `salary-calculation` queries pick up withdrawal accruals automatically (filter is `salaryPaymentId: null, reversedAt: null` + `lessonDate` range), so no special-case logic. The withdrawal day as `lessonDate` puts the accrual in the payroll period that is open when it is written.

#### "Where did this payment go?" — replay the ledger, never re-derive it

`common/finance/ledger-replay.ts` is the ONE engine behind the payment card on the student "To'lovlar" tab. It exists because the previous `computePaymentDestination` kept its own FIFO queue of payments and funded only the deductions that came AFTER each one. A lesson taken on credit hit an empty queue, fell through the loop, and no later payment ever covered it — so money that had already cleared that debt was reported as "remainderInBalance". **540 of 569 students showed a wrong card**, and one read theirs as "I have 233 339 so'm" while sitting at −33 325.

The answer was already in the database. Every student-scoped row stores `balanceBefore` / `balanceAfter` under `lockStudent`'s `SELECT … FOR UPDATE`, and a production audit found **0 violations of `balanceAfter − balanceBefore === amount` across 39 516 rows** (except `EXPENSE`, which hardcodes 0/0 and is not student-scoped). A reporting layer that recomputes that from `amount` alone is rebuilding a truth it could read.

Four rules, each load-bearing — do not relax them one at a time:

- **Every balance-moving row is in the walk.** `where: { studentId, companyId, amount: { not: 0 } }` — no type list. `ADJUSTMENT` alone was 316 rows across 254 students the old walk could not see; `DISCOUNT_ADJUSTMENT`, `DEBT_WRITE_OFF`, `MOCK_EXAM_FEE` add 37 more. `amount: { not: 0 }` drops only `LESSON_CONSUMPTION`, the one row type written without a balance lock.
- **NO reversal filter.** `reverseTransaction` writes the counter-row with `type: original.type` and `reversedAt: null`, so filtering `reversedAt: null` keeps the undo and drops the original. Include both and the pair nets to zero: **0 chain breaks over 28 950 production rows, versus 99 when the original is filtered out.** This is the opposite of `lesson-coverage.helper.ts`, which counts LESSONS rather than money and therefore excludes **both** halves (`reversedAt: null AND reversedTransactionId: null`). Same for `getBalanceSummary` — a summary answers "what stands today".
- **Signed amounts, never `Math.abs`.** `amount > 0` credits, `< 0` debits. `Math.abs` turned those positive counter-rows into fresh lesson charges (124 rows, 4 572 301 so'm, 65 students) in both the card and `getBalanceSummary`.
- **FAIL-CLOSED.** If the replayed chain ever disagrees with the stored balances, `reconciled: false` comes back and the UI renders the balance facts only. The defect being replaced was a plausible-looking wrong number; a patched-up number would re-legitimise it.

Money is allocated at **per-lesson slice** granularity (`splitLessonSlices` + `CycleCoverage.consumedDates`), not per deduction batch — otherwise a payment funding part of a 5-lesson batch inherits the whole batch's date range (one student's 21.07 card read "21.07 — 04.08" instead of "21.07 — 30.07").

A lesson deduction is **all-or-nothing**: `lesson-billing.service.ts` never bills more than the balance in `FULL_CYCLE`/`PARTIAL`, and in `SINGLE_UNCOVERED` it bills the full lesson and drives the balance negative. So `|amount| > balanceBefore` means the lesson is entirely unpaid. Every OTHER debit type (`REFUND`, `BALANCE_WITHDRAWAL`, …) funds partially, exactly as the stored balance shows.

Invariants, and the one that is impossible: **`Σ remainder === Student.balance` cannot hold** — a negative balance is not a sum of non-negative remainders. The real one is `Σ unspent − Σ outstandingDebt === Student.balance`. Guard: `scripts/audit-payment-destination.ts` (read-only, exits non-zero on any violation; **684/684 students clean**). `scripts/show-payment-cards.ts` prints one student's cards as the UI renders them.

**The card's vocabulary is part of the fix.** `remainderInBalance` is gone and must not come back under any name that claims a holding: it is what let a student read "233 339 qoldi" while owing money. The field is `unspent`, rendered as "Sarflanmagan qoldiq", never green while the balance is negative, with today's real balance on the latest payment card.

Deliberately NOT merged into this engine: `attributeIncome` (`common/finance/income-attribution.ts`, called by `getIncomeMonthAttribution` in `reports-financial.service.ts` and by the To'lovlar page). It filters reversals on purpose — it reports _corrected_ history, while the card reports what the admin actually saw that day. Two questions, two engines, each documented; a shared flag would hide the difference.

#### Lesson Trail (`GET /transactions/student/:id/lesson-trail`)

Per-student "where did each so'm go for lessons?" report. Strictly scoped to `LESSON_DEDUCTION` (prepaid-batch allocation rows) and `LESSON_CONSUMPTION` (per-lesson use rows) — money-flow types (PAYMENT/REFUND/ADJUSTMENT/INITIAL_BALANCE) are filtered out at the service level. Paginated (`page`, `pageSize`). Returns rows in ASC order (chronological story) enriched with attendance metadata (date, group, course) and reversal markers. No screen reads it now: the «Darslar» tab reads `GET /students/:id/lessons-overview` (`getLessonsOverview`: month blocks from the first monthly charge on, pack cycles before — ADR-0062).

#### Student transactions list (`GET /transactions/student/:id`)

Used by the "To'lovlar" tab. Accepts a `types` query parameter — a comma-separated list of `TransactionType` values (e.g. `?types=PAYMENT,REFUND,ADJUSTMENT,INITIAL_BALANCE,BALANCE_WITHDRAWAL,LESSON_DEDUCTION`) — so the tab can request only the rows that move the balance. Validated against the enum at the DTO boundary; invalid tokens reject. The legacy single-`type` parameter still works as a fallback for one type.

**Tab overlap on `LESSON_DEDUCTION` (intentional).** `LESSON_DEDUCTION` is a real money-flow row — it decreases the student balance — so it appears on **both** the "To'lovlar" tab (so a balance drop is never unexplained, e.g. a payment immediately consumed by retroactive billing) **and** the "Darslar" tab (where it shows which prepaid batch covered which lessons). It is the **one** `TransactionType` deliberately shared between the two tabs. `LESSON_CONSUMPTION` (amount=0, no balance movement) stays exclusive to the "Darslar" tab. Every other type belongs to exactly one tab.

**Every row carries `reversal`** (`transactions/reversal-view.ts`): `{ kind: 'reversed', at, reason }` on a row a later cancellation undid, `{ kind: 'undo', originalAt, originalAmount, reason }` on the counter-row that undid it, `null` otherwise. Both rows stay on the list because the balance column is a running total; without the mark the original read as a charge still standing (2 375 pairs across 538 students on 06.10.2026, most from the 26.09 monthly switch). `reason` is the counter-row's description without the `Bekor qilindi: ` prefix, `null` when only the row id was written; some reasons are reworded there, never by rewriting rows: the legacy English ones (Payme's «Payme CancelTransaction» reads as «Payme to'lovni bekor qildi», ADR-0088), and the monthly switch's «Oylik to'lovga o'tish migratsiyasi — 2026-09», read as «Oylik to'lovga o'tildi, bu dars puli sentabr oylik to'loviga kirdi» (CEO, 06.10.2026). `GET /student-portal/payments` returns the same field and, since then, only rows with `amount <> 0` — the lesson consumption markers filled its 20-row window with «+0 so'm» lines.

#### Enrollment Lifecycle Prepaid Refund (`EnrollmentBillingService`)

When an enrollment closes (TRANSFERRED or DROPPED), unused prepaid lessons are converted back to balance. **Original** `perLessonCost` (from the most recent unreversed `LESSON_DEDUCTION.metadata.perLessonCost`) is used so course price changes after the deduction don't affect the refund. Falls back to the current course price for legacy rows without metadata.

- `removeFromGroup()`: refund + flip to DROPPED in one tx.
- Group deletion and a group's own CANCELLED/COMPLETED: the same refund for every enrollment they close, inside that operation's transaction — one failed refund rolls the whole operation back (ADR-0041). The branch, course and student cascades instead refund each enrollment in its own transaction and log a failure, so one bad enrollment does not hold up a batch of hundreds.
- Transfer (`enrollToGroup` with existing enrollment): refund old enrollment + close TRANSFERRED + create new enrollment + state log — all in a single Serializable transaction so we never end up with prepaid stranded on a closed enrollment.
- **A MONTHLY transfer also cuts the old enrollment's current-month charge** back to the lessons held up to the transfer (`MonthlyChargeService.reverseChargeForDeparture`, the same call `removeFromGroup` makes). Without it the student paid the old course's FULL month on top of the new course's prorated share — a mid-October Standart → Intensive switch billed 853 636 instead of 611 331 (CEO 21.09, answer 13: each course at its own price over its own lesson count). `refundPrepaidToBalance` alone is a no-op on a MONTHLY enrollment.
- **Contract 6.2 (ADR-0043, replaced by ADR-0044): a student's own departure after more than 40% of the month keeps the month's charge; a completed level does not.** `reverseChargeForDeparture` takes a `policy`: `STUDENT_CANCELLED` (the default of a group removal and of an expulsion), `LEVEL_COMPLETED` (the student finished the level — certificate or next level later: the contract is fulfilled, not cancelled, so the unheld lessons come back; contract 3.4), `CENTER_INITIATIVE` (the unheld lessons come back — what every caller that passes nothing gets, i.e. freeze, transfer, group/branch/course closings, group deletion and a card archive) and `QUALITY_CLAIM` (the whole month back). The rule is ONE pure function, `policyRelease` (`billing/departure-policy.ts`), shared by the write and `previewDepartureOutcomes`, which backs `GET /students/:id/departure-preview` and the «Pul (shartnoma bo'yicha)» block in both dialogs — never recompute a departure's money anywhere else. Share = covered lessons held through the departure day ÷ covered lessons, frozen-out dates excluded from both; «more than» is strict; the threshold is `payment.noRefundAfterPercent` (company-level, default 40); departures before `CONTRACT_62_START_DAY` (01.10.2026) keep the old rule; a month with nothing left to return is never reported as withheld. `LEVEL_COMPLETED` is open to everyone who may remove a student (`OPEN_DEPARTURE_POLICIES`) and refused on an expulsion (400); only a CEO or Branch Director may send `CENTER_INITIATIVE` or `QUALITY_CLAIM` (`assertMayChooseDeparturePolicy`, read from the database — ADR-0028, 403 otherwise); a policy with any status but EXPELLED is a 400. A card ARCHIVE is not a departure (a record made by mistake) and keeps the old rule. The student's and the group's history rows carry a `pul` line (`departureMoneyNote`); teacher accruals are never touched by any policy.
- **A MONTHLY return is charged only for the lessons after the return day**, whether or not its month already has a charge. A student frozen when a month's charge run went by has no row for that month; `restoreChargeForReturn` then creates it at the unfreeze, and the daily run (`createChargesForPeriod`) starts any later charge after the same day (`chargeStartDate`). Before this the unfreeze charged nothing and the next 04:00 run billed the whole month from the join date, frozen weeks included. The daily run reads the return from `Enrollment.statusChangedAt`: on an ACTIVE enrollment it is the moment it last came back to ACTIVE, so write `statusChangedAt` only together with a real status change.
- **The enroll dialog asks the server what adding the student would charge (A3.4).** `GET /students/:id/enroll-preview?groupId=&startDate=` (`StudentEnrollPreviewService`; CEO / Branch Director / Administrator, the roles of `POST /students/:id/enroll`, the call it previews). `startDate` is the optional `YYYY-MM-DD` the dialog would send, today when omitted, as in `enrollToGroup`. It writes nothing, checks the student's branch, then the group's (the enroll call checks only the group's branch: a student of another branch is refused, a student with none takes the group's), and answers `{ paymentModel, coursePrice, lessonPaymentCount, discountPercent, firstMonth, balance, transferRelease, payable }`. The client prints these and adds nothing up (`client/src/components/students/enroll-preview-block.tsx`).
  - **MONTHLY quotes the FIRST charge the student will actually get**, which is not always this month's. The month is that of the later of today and the charge's first day (`chargeStartDate`: the chosen start, never before the group's own start): a first day in a later month is charged by that month's run, any other by the enroll call itself, in the current Tashkent month (`chargeMidMonthJoin`). When that month leaves nothing to charge — no lesson from the first day on, or every one already paid by another charge of the student in the same group — the quote rolls forward ONCE, to the next month, never further. Only a MONTHLY course in an ACTIVE group rolls: the service's gate mirrors the refusals of `previewChargeForNewEnrollment` (not MONTHLY, group not ACTIVE), so that past it that method's `null` can only mean "nothing to charge" — a refusal added to one must be added to the other. `firstMonth` is null for a pack course (it has no first month), and for a MONTHLY one when the group is not ACTIVE yet or two months in a row have nothing to charge.
  - **The amount is the write's own.** `MonthlyChargeService.previewChargeForNewEnrollment` and `createChargeForEnrollment` both call `planChargeAmounts` (the month's plan, the proration and the student's discount), so the dialog cannot quote a sum the write does not charge: change that arithmetic there and nowhere else. The quote never adds excused credit, because a new enrollment has none carried over from an earlier month; it is the discounted, prorated price.
  - **`payable = max(0, due − (balance + transferRelease))`**, where `due` is `firstMonth.amount` for MONTHLY (0 when `firstMonth` is null) and, for LESSON_PACK, the pack price at the student's discount (`applyDiscount`), the amount pack billing deducts for a full cycle.
  - **`transferRelease` is what a transfer gives back from the old group's month** before the new group is charged. A student ACTIVE in another group is transferred: `enrollToGroup` closes that enrollment (one live ACTIVE enrollment per student) and calls `reverseChargeForDeparture` with no policy — CENTER_INITIATIVE, and never contract 3.5's trial lesson, which is for a student leaving. The quote takes it from `previewReleaseForDeparture`, that call's read-only twin, not from `previewDepartureOutcomes`, which applies the trial lesson under every policy and would quote a first-timer's whole month. 0 when the student is in no other group, or when the old month has no standing charge or nothing left to give back. A pack enrollment's prepaid refund (`refundPrepaidToBalance`) is left out, as no pack courses remain, which is why the dialog still calls the figure «To'lash kerak (taxminan)».
- **A join month is charged when the enrollment is written, by every door.** The admin door does it in its own transaction (`StudentEnrollmentService.chargeMidMonthJoin`). The Telegram self-registration writes the enrollment itself and cannot import `BillingModule` (BillingModule → TelegramDigestModule → TelegramModule), so `registerStudentFromTelegram` emits `STUDENT_SELF_ENROLLED` and `SelfEnrollmentChargeListener` calls `MonthlyChargeService.chargeJoinMonth`. Left to the 04:00 daily run, a sign-up on a month's last lesson day was never billed for it, because the next run already bills the new month (30.09.2026: five students). A new door that writes an enrollment charges its join month one of these two ways.

#### Payment Reverse Block

`payments-write.service.ts:reverse()` refuses if any non-reversed `LESSON_CONSUMPTION` exists for the student dated AFTER the payment landed, with «To'lov allaqachon darslarga sarflangan — bekor qilib bo'lmaydi. Pulni o'quvchiga «Pul qaytarish» orqali qaytaring.» The funds are already spent on lessons — admins must use the formal `Refund` flow (which has proper math for partial completion + deductions). Force-reverse is intentionally not provided to prevent ledger drift.

**A reason is required for a human caller (ADR-0088).** `POST /payments/:id/reverse` takes `ReversePaymentDto { reason }` (trimmed, not empty, 500 characters) and the service repeats the check when `performedById` is set: 400 «Bekor qilish sababini yozing». System callers (Payme `CancelTransaction`, no user) are exempt; the amount correction sends its own reason. The To'lovlar drawer's «Bekor qilish» (`money.undo`, CEO by default) is the only screen that calls it.

#### Payment Amount Correction

`payments-write.service.ts:correctAmount()` (`POST /payments/:id/correct`) fixes a wrong amount on a manual payment (e.g. cashier typed 4 000 000 instead of 400 000). It is **reverse + re-post**, not an in-place edit — the append-only ledger rule holds. The original payment becomes `REVERSED`; a fresh payment is created at the correct amount. The two steps run as separate Serializable transactions (not one atomic unit); if the re-post fails, a precise recovery message is surfaced.

- **The re-post carries the link (ADR-0088).** `correctAmount` passes the internal `options.correctsPaymentId` (not in the DTO) to `create`, which writes `Payment.correctsPaymentId` (unique, on the NEW payment, the old payment's id) in the same transaction as the new payment. It is the ONLY link between the two: the To'lovlar page reads `correctedBy` / `correctsPayment` from the column and never guesses from the note text. If the re-post fails there is no link and the old row reads as a plain cancelled payment. Corrections from before were linked once by `scripts/link-payment-corrections.ts`.
- **Roles**: CEO, BD, Admin (`@Roles` on the endpoint excludes Cashier).
- It also fixes a wrong **payment method** (`method` optional in `CorrectPaymentDto` — when omitted the original method is kept). Amount and method can be corrected together or independently.
- **Method-only correction is an in-place update, NOT reverse + re-post.** When the amount is unchanged (only the method differs), the balance never moves, so `correctAmount()` early-returns after a single Serializable tx that just updates `Payment.method` (+ `recordUpdate` audit + CEO alert). The ledger (`Transaction`) stores balances, not the method, so no ledger row changes. **Consequence:** the "funds already spent on lessons" guard does NOT apply to a method-only fix — a mis-recorded method (e.g. CASH → TRANSFER) can be relabelled even after the money was consumed by lessons. The reverse+re-post path (and its consumption guard) is reached only when the **amount** changes.
- **Guardrails** (all enforced in the service): only `ADMIN_MANUAL` source (gateway amounts are provider-owned); only `COMPLETED` status; amount and/or method must differ; non-CEO callers bound to a **72h window** after the payment landed (`ADMIN_CORRECTION_WINDOW_HOURS`, CEOs bypass); **on an amount change** blocked when funds were already spent on lessons (`LESSON_CONSUMPTION` exists) — that needs the CEO lesson-deduction unwind flow.
- A `reason` is **mandatory only when the amount changes** (an amount fix must be explained in the audit trail). A **method-only** correction (money unchanged, e.g. CASH → TRANSFER) needs no reason — the service enforces this. When given, the reason lands in the audit trail and the re-posted payment's `note` records the previous amount + reason.
- **Student notifications**: two instant Telegram messages (ADR-0065) — `payment.reversed` (old payment rolled back) then `payment.received` (new payment posted).
- **CEO alert**: when a non-CEO performs the correction, `payment.corrected` is emitted → `NotificationEventsListener` notifies all company CEOs (DB + SSE + Push instantly, Telegram through the 20:00 digest; `NotificationType.SYSTEM`).

#### Status Transitions (centralized in `src/common/finance/status-transitions.ts`)

- `assertValidTransition(entityType, map, fromStatus, toStatus)` — throws `BadRequestException` if invalid
- Used across: payments (reverse), refunds (hand-over, cancel), salary (approve/pay), contracts (status change)

#### RBAC for Financial Features

| ------------------------------------------------ | :-: | :-: | :---: | :-----: | :-----: |
| Create payment                                   | ✅  | ✅  |  ✅   |   ✅    |   ❌    |
| Reverse payment                                  | ✅  | ❌  |  ❌   |   ❌    |   ❌    |
| Correct payment amount                           | ✅  | ✅  |  ✅   |   ❌    |   ❌    |

| «Qaytariladigan pul» reads (`/refundable/*`)     | ✅  | ✅  |  ✅   |   ✅    |   ❌    |
| Reverse refund                                   | ✅  | ❌  |  ❌   |   ❌    |   ❌    |
| Create expense                                   | ✅  | ✅  |  ❌   |   ❌    |   ❌    |
| Financial reports                                | ✅  | ✅  |  ❌   |   ❌    |   ❌    |

| Salary page reads (`/payments/salary`)           | ✅  | ✅  |  ❌   |   ❌    |   ❌    |
| «Markaz qoplagani» (salary page tab)             | ✅  | ✅  |  ❌   |   ❌    |   ❌    |

### DaF progress (`src/daf/`, ADR-0114, ADR-0115, ADR-0116, ADR-0117, ADR-0124)

- **Every attempt goes through `recordAttempt`** (`daf/shared/attempt-writer.ts`): the `DafAttempt` row and its `DafStudentDay` day total (`+points`, `+1 attempt`, Postgres `ON CONFLICT … DO UPDATE`) commit in one transaction. `attempt-writer.single-source.spec.ts` fails on any other write of either table.
- **`DafStudentDay` is derived** (student × Tashkent day × `groupKey`, `''` for no group, never NULL). `DafAttempt` stays the source of truth, points included. After anything deletes or rewrites attempts, or after the points formula changes, run `scripts/rebuild-daf-student-day.ts --apply`; `scripts/audit-daf-student-day.ts` (read-only) must then report 0. In a deploy the old instance can still save answers while the new one runs the migration, so run the rebuild (dry run, then `--apply` on a difference) once the old instance is gone, then the audit (ADR-0114).
- **`FortschrittService` reads only `DafStudentDay`** for total, streak and both weekly rankings; the ranking rules of the 06.09 design are unchanged.
- **The session end answers with `natija`** (`seansNatijasiXavfsiz`): points of the session, streak, `serieYangi`, rank before/after for group and centre. A failure there returns `null` and never fails the save.
- **The daily goal is a choice, not derived (ADR-0115).** `DafStudentSetting` (one row per student, no row = `ORTA`) is written only by `PATCH student-portal/lernen/maqsad` (upsert). The points (40/80/160) are `MAQSAD_BALL` in `daf/uebung/punkte.ts`; the client sheet's numbers are tested against that file. `GET fortschritt` answers `maqsad` with no extra query for the goal level and today's points: today's points and the goal level are CTEs of the `oquvchi-raqamlari` SQL, and «an open lesson exists» (`bugunBoshmi`, `fortschritt/maqsad.ts`, the path's lock chain) is read only when `faelligeWoerter == 0`.
- **A badge is given once and never taken back (ADR-0115).** The one writer of `DafStudentBadge` is `grantBadges` (`daf/nishon/nishon-berish.ts`, `INSERT … ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING RETURNING key`); `nishon-berish.single-source.spec.ts` fails on any other write. The catalogue is code (`nishon-katalogi.ts`), the client builds the name from the key. Badges are granted only at a session's end, inside `seansNatijasi` (`natija.yangiNishonlar`); a failure there gives `[]` and never nulls `natija`. `GET nishonlar` writes nothing. Students who practised before S2: `scripts/daf-badges-backfill.ts` (dry run, then `--apply --expect=<N>`).
- **The weekly league only promotes (ADR-0116).** `DafStudentLeague` (one row per student, no row = Bronza, tier 0–4) only rises — the promotion upsert uses `GREATEST`. Rooms are weekly (`DafLeagueRoom` + `DafLeagueEntry`, one room per student per week); the rules (capacity 30, even Monday split of `ceil(n/25)` by `hash(studentId, weekStart)`, first 5 with ≥ 50 points promote only in a room of ≥ 15, none from Olmos) are pure functions in `daf/liga/liga-qoidalari.ts`. Points are never stored during the week: rooms read `DafStudentDay` (all group keys). Only two files write these tables: `daf/liga/liga-xona.ts` (`joinRoom`: room and entry) and `liga-hisob.ts` (`settleWeek`: all four); `liga.single-source.spec.ts` fails on any other write (one allowlisted load-test seed, `scripts/load/daf-yuklama-s3-seed.ts`, writes `DafStudentLeague`). `GET liga` / `GET guruhlar` write nothing.
- **`settleWeek` is idempotent and self-healing (ADR-0116).** It claims rooms with `UPDATE … WHERE "settledAt" IS NULL RETURNING`, one transaction per company and week, under the company advisory lock `LIGA_HISOB_KALIT` taken EXCLUSIVELY: the Monday 00:05 cron (`LigaCron`) waits for it, a session end tries it (`pg_try_advisory_xact_lock`). `joinRoom` takes it SHARED, then the tier's room lock exclusively, and its first statement is `set_config('lock_timeout', '2s', true)` — if a settle starts between the state read and the join, the join times out after at most 2 s, rolls back, and the student joins at the next session end; `liganiYangilaXavfsiz` does not call `joinRoom` at all while an older unsettled week remains (`kechikkan` still true after `settleDue`, e.g. the lock was busy), so a promoted student is never seated in the old tier's room; never remove it (without it a 22 s settle made all 40 test joins fail — 10 held pool connections for 20 s — and plain reads waited 20 s). A week the cron missed is settled at the next session end (`liganiYangilaXavfsiz` → `settleDue`, oldest week first, only when an older unsettled week exists), inside the safe wrapper: a failure never nulls `natija`.
- **The group table is the average of current members (ADR-0116).** `guruhlar-jadvali.ts`: ACTIVE members of ACTIVE live groups, points with `groupKey` = the group (as the group ranking), ≥ 3 members, average DESC then group id ASC, TOP 10 + own group; not limited to a branch (06.09 §6.1). Keep the SQL shape: sum the week per (student, group) first, then join the members (the other order cost 338 ms p95 against 163–169 ms). The week's result is written once to `DafGroupWeek` by `settleWeek`.
- **League badges are state-based (ADR-0116).** `LIGA_KUMUSH/OLTIN/YOQUT/OLMOS`, `XONA_GOLIB` and `GURUH_BIRINCHI` go through the same `grantBadges` at a session's end. `fortschritt/fortschritt.service.ts` is already 732 lines: new league logic goes into `daf/liga/`, not into it.
- **Reminders are instant, go through `SmsService` and skip a disconnected chat (ADR-0117).** `daf/eslatma/`: the 19:30 evening reminder (Mon–Sat, not on a company-wide holiday) and the Monday summary (09:00, retried hourly until 18:00 while last week has an unsettled room) are on ADR-0025's instant list and are sent only by `EslatmaYuborish` through `SmsService.sendToStudent(…, 'AUTO', …, { replyMarkup })` — never a new `.sendMessage(`. Who gets one is the pure rules of `eslatma-qoidalari.ts`, mirrored by one SQL each in `eslatma-auditoriya.ts` (`scripts/load/daf-yuklama-s4-paritet.ts` compares them); `telegramDisconnectedAt IS NULL` is required — a reminder is not important enough for ADR-0066's «send anyway». `DAF_ESLATMALAR` (`off` default | `log` | `on`) is read on every run.
- **`DafReminderLog` is claimed before the send (ADR-0117).** `eslatmaniEgalla` is `INSERT … ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING RETURNING id` on `(studentId, kind, sentOn)`: only the call that made the row sends, a second cron instance gets `null`. The row starts `FAILED` and becomes `SENT`/`FAILED`; a `FAILED` row is never retried and never counts toward the yesterday / 4-in-7-days / summary-today rules. Only `eslatma-jurnali.ts` writes the table and only `eslatma-sozlama.ts` (`eslatmalarniSaqla`) writes `remindersEnabled` — `eslatma-jurnali.single-source.spec.ts` fails on any other WRITE of the table or of the column (readers are not flagged; a spread `data: dto` is not seen). The bot's `dr:off:<studentId>` button works only from that student's own `telegramChatId`.
- **Reminder known limits (ADR-0117).** Every reminder writes one `SmsMessage` and one `EntityHistory` row (at most ≈ 43k/month each at 2 000 students, nothing purges them); a Telegram 429 marks the rest of the audience `FAILED` for the day (no back-off); the 60 ms pacing runs in a `finally` after every claimed row, also when `sendToStudent` throws; the evening streak scan reads the full history (800 ms on a 2-year copy, 408 ms with a 60-day window and fallback, same result — not applied yet); the 90-day purge runs only in `on` mode; in `log` mode a ready Monday logs the audience every hour 09:00–18:00. The CEO sample send (`scripts/daf-eslatma-sinov.ts --send`) is a manual step before `DAF_ESLATMALAR=on`.
- **The teacher competition view is read-only (ADR-0124).** `src/daf/ustoz/` writes nothing (guarded by `musobaqa-ustoz.read-only.spec.ts`). The group card reuses `guruhlarYigindisi`/`guruhlarniSarala` (S3) for the current week and `DafGroupWeek` for past weeks; «not practised» uses total points across groups; `GET /groups/:id/musobaqa` follows the `app-activity` group guard (`assertCallerMayTouchGroup`). Concurrent requests of the same company+week share one in-flight read of the centre-wide group table (`yigindi()`), dropped as soon as it settles — no cache.

### Lesson ratings (`src/lesson-ratings/`, ADR-0091)

- **Who is asked:** `LessonRatingSenderService` (`@Cron('0 */10 7-22 * * 1-6')`, Tashkent) reads today's ended lessons through `loadEndedLessonsToday` (`attendance/shared/load-ended-lessons.ts`, shared with «Dars bo'ldimi?») and asks, 10–180 minutes after the lesson's end, every PRESENT/LATE student whose card is live and whose `telegramChatId` is set and not disconnected (ADR-0066). Off while `lessonRating.enabled` (company-level, default false) is off. A failure for one student (or one lesson) is logged and does not stop the others.
- **One row per lesson and student** (`LessonRating`, unique `groupId + studentId + date`), written BEFORE the message, so two instances never send twice (`P2002` → skip). A refused send is `FAILED` and never retried. `messageId` is set after a successful send; a row with `messageId` null is tolerated (the post-send update may fail — the student still got the message and can rate) but the report counts it as «not delivered». `teacherIds` and `branchId` are frozen at send time (a `LessonTeacherOverride` wins over `GroupTeacher`).
- **Bot:** `LessonRatingTelegramHandler` registers one `Composer` through `useBeforeScenes` (as tasks do): `lr:<id>:<1-5>` — the pressing chat must be the row's, 48 hours, first score wins (`updateMany where score: null`); a score ≤ 3 sends a `force_reply` «Nima yoqmadi?» whose id is `promptMessageId`; a text reply to it is the comment (first one wins, 500 chars). Everything else goes `next()`. Pure rules: `rating-rules.ts`; aggregates: `rating-math.ts`.
- **Reads are the CEO's alone:** every `/lesson-ratings/*` route is `@CeoOnly()` (identity, never a capability); the three GETs take `@BranchScope()`, `PATCH /lesson-ratings/settings` is `COMPANY_WIDE`. `GET /lesson-ratings/teachers/:id` looks the teacher up within the caller's company (`companyId`), so an id from another company answers 404. A rating is counted for every teacher of its lesson, so the teachers' rows do not add up to the total. `GET /lesson-ratings/summary` also answers `distribution` (counts of 1…5, index 0 = score 1), `lowCommented` (ratings ≤ `LOW_SCORE_MAX` with a comment) and `best` / `worst` (`bestAndWorst` in `rating-math.ts`: among teachers with an average; `worst` is null when only one is rated or all averages are equal; ties keep the first).
- **Fully anonymous (ADR-0111):** reports are fully anonymous — no student name or id in any response (`low`, `teacher`, `summary`); `studentId` lives in `LessonRating` only for de-duplication. `GET /lesson-ratings/low` rows carry `commentState` (`WRITTEN` / `PENDING` / `NOT_WRITTEN`, computed from `sentAt` + 48h via `isWithinWindow`) and accept `?commented=1` (only rows with a comment); summary teacher rows carry `commented`.

### Tasks (`src/tasks/`)

A task is not a comment (ADR-0074; spec `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-07-topshiriqlar-design.md`). Tables: `Task`, `TaskParticipant` (`ASSIGNEE` / `WATCHER`), `TaskStep`, `TaskEvent` (discussion and history in one feed), `TaskOutbox`, `TaskFile` (ADR-0086). Routes: `TasksController` (`/tasks`, `@AnyStaff()`; the policy narrows further).

- **One door.** Every write to a `Task*` table goes through `src/tasks/` (`TasksService`, `lesson-task.ts`, `task-user-lifecycle.listener.ts`, `task-recurrences.service.ts`, `task-recurrence-run.service.ts`, `task-files.service.ts` for `TaskFile`) or `scripts/migrate-comment-tasks.ts`. `task-write.single-source.spec.ts` scans `src/` and fails on `prisma|tx|db.task*.create|update|delete…` anywhere else. A write from another module would skip the policy, the event log and the outbox: add a method to `TasksService` instead.
- **Policy** (`task-policy.ts`, pure functions; roles come from the DB, ADR-0028). The assignee's HIGHEST role must be on the caller's ladder: CEO → roles 1–5, Branch Director → 2–5 in own branches, Administrator → 3–5 in own branches, Teacher / Cashier → themselves only. A watcher is anyone assignable, oneself, or someone more senior (an administrator may add the CEO as watcher; they only read). `resolveAccess` gives `canView` / `canManage` / `canWork`; the author, the CEO and a Branch Director of the task's branch manage it.
- **Transitions** (`task-transitions.ts`, `checkTransition`): NEW → IN_PROGRESS → IN_REVIEW → DONE. The assignee sends to review; the author or a manager accepts (`POST /tasks/:id/review` ACCEPT → DONE) or returns it (RETURN → IN_PROGRESS, reason required, `returnedCount + 1`); cancelling is the manager's. A task whose author is its only assignee skips review and goes to DONE. A manager cannot bypass review through `changeStatus`: it refuses a non-assignee (400 «Qabul qilish yoki qaytarish tugmasidan foydalaning»). DONE / CANCELLED never reopen (`POST /tasks/:id/duplicate`). A task with `kind` ≠ `MANUAL` is closed only by its source (400 otherwise); the assignee can only start it, and the first to act claims it (`task-claim.ts`, `claimSystemTask`). A task with `requiresPhoto` goes to review only with an IMAGE added by an ASSIGNEE after its last return or its creation, not deleted (`hasFreshPhoto`, read with the status transaction's own client in `changeStatusTx`; 400 `PHOTO_REQUIRED_MESSAGE` «Tekshiruvga yuborish uchun rasm qo'shing»); the giver's reference photo and a watcher's picture do not count, and a self-task closes without one. `requiresPhoto` is set at creation only (`POST /tasks`); a recurring task refuses it (400).
- **Due rules** (`task-due.ts`): a date with no time = 18:00 Asia/Tashkent; a manual `dueAt` must fall 08:00–22:00, not on a Sunday and not on a holiday of the task's branch (400); a system task's due moves to the next working day (`shiftSystemDueAt`).
- **Outbox** (`task-outbox.service.ts`): `scheduleTaskOutbox` rewrites the time-based rows (`REMINDER` one hour before `dueAt`, `OVERDUE` at it) on create and on assignee, due or priority changes — an `INAPP` row and, except for «Dars bo'ldimi?», a `TELEGRAM` row per recipient. A `TELEGRAM` row that falls in the night quiet (22:00–08:00 Tashkent, `task-quiet-hours.ts`) is written for the next 08:00; an `URGENT` task's never waits. A Telegram `REMINDER` whose (shifted) send time would be at or after `dueAt` is not written: the `OVERDUE` notice says it. The rewrite touches `REMINDER`/`OVERDUE` only: Telegram notices held for the morning or a retry (`NOTICE`, the planned message in `payload`) stay. The time rows are one per task, person, channel and kind (the partial unique index `TaskOutbox_time_row_key`, hand-written in the migration, which `createMany({ skipDuplicates })` leans on); `NOTICE` rows have no such key, several notices of one task may wait for one person. A per-minute cron drains `INAPP` to the bell, SSE and push, dropping rows of closed tasks and inactive users; `TaskTelegramOutbox` drains `TELEGRAM` (below). Sent rows, and rows that failed three times, older than 30 days go at 03:00 Tashkent (`TaskOutboxService.purge`). Instant events are `task.*` (`task-events.ts`, `task.assigned` carries `created: true` for a new task), fanned out by `task-notify.listener.ts` (bell) and `telegram/task-telegram.listener.ts` (Telegram) from ONE plan, `task-notify-plan.ts` (`Notice.telegram`). A task created before ADR-0078 has no Telegram time rows until its due date, priority or assignees change.
- **Telegram** (`src/tasks/telegram/`, ADR-0078 + ADR-0089; specs `2026-10-07-topshiriqlar-design.md` §6 and `2026-10-14-topshiriq-tirik-xabar-design.md`): task notices go from the main bot when they happen, not in the 20:00 digest. `TasksModule` imports `TelegramModule` (one way: `src/telegram` imports nothing from tasks; the staff menu owns only the `'tk:list'` string).
  - **Who:** `Notice.telegram` — assignees on assign / add / move / return / accept, the author on review, the removed assignee, author + assignees (not watchers) on a comment, watchers only on «Bajarildi» / «Bekor qilindi» (a cancellation reaches Telegram through the watchers alone; the assignees see it in the bell); never the actor; never «Dars bo'ldimi?» (`LESSON_QUESTION`). A watcher added or removed, a due change and a plain status change are bell-only. **A status notice (`STATUS_NOTICES`: ASSIGNED, ADDED, MOVED, REVIEW, ACCEPTED, RETURNED, DONE, CANCELLED) is only ever the person's FIRST live card** (ADR-0089): `TaskTelegramSender.send` skips it (`has card`) when a `CARD` row for that task, person and chat exists, and otherwise sends the live card as it is now — not the notice's text; the «Live cards» bullet below edits it from then on. `REMOVED` never sends (`live card only`): the person's card is edited to «Topshiriqdan olib tashlandingiz». COMMENT, REMINDER and OVERDUE stay new `NOTICE` messages. The recipient must be an ACTIVE (`status`, `isActive`) sign-in staff account whose `telegramChatId` no OTHER live sign-in staff account shares, active or inactive (`staffChatOf`, `task-telegram-view.ts`: the owner count runs on `staffLinkedToChatWhere`, so an INACTIVE account on the same chat blocks it too), checked at send time. `GET /tasks/assignable` flags the same people with `telegramLinked` (`usersWithTelegram`, one query for the list), so the picker's «Telegram ulanmagan» badge and the send rule cannot disagree.
  - **When:** `TaskTelegramListener` right after the commit; inside 22:00–08:00 a non-`URGENT` notice becomes a `TaskOutbox` `TELEGRAM`/`NOTICE` row for 08:00 (only when the person has a sendable chat then), and a transient failure a retry row (`attempts = 1`, `retry_after` or 60 s, moved out of the night quiet unless `URGENT`). `TaskTelegramOutbox` drains every minute at second 30, 50 rows, oldest `sendAfter` first. Each row is CLAIMED before it is sent (a conditional `updateMany` pushes `sendAfter` 5 minutes ahead), so an old and a new instance during a deploy do not both send it (a run stops after half the 5-minute claim; a send that hangs longer than the claim can still go twice), and a run that dies lets the row come back. `REMINDER`/`OVERDUE` go only while the task is open and the person is still on it (`skipReason`; a `REMINDER` also not once the due date has passed); a morning-queued ASSIGNED / ADDED / MOVED notice is dropped once the task is closed (`task closed`), and a queued status notice is dropped by the sender when the person got a `CARD` in the meantime; a row nobody can send (task gone or closed, person not on it, no sendable chat) is closed with a note in `lastError`, never retried. A 429 waits `retry_after` (no attempt counted) and ends the run; a permanent or malformed-message error, and a bot that is off (no `TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN`), kill the row (`attempts = 3`); a transient one counts an attempt and backs off `60 s × 2^attempts`: 60 s after the first failure, 2 min after the second, and the third failure kills the row (a listener retry row starts at `attempts = 1`, so the immediate failure is the first; there is no 4-minute wait).
  - **Message** (`task-telegram-text.ts`, pure). Two kinds (ADR-0089). The **live card** `renderLiveCard(view, viewerId, { now, openUrl })`: a headline from the task's state AND the reader (spec 2026-10-14 §3.3 — «🔄 Jarayonda · Siz boshladingiz» for the actor, «Karimov B. boshladi» for everyone else, «Tekshiruvga keldi» for the author, «Topshiriqdan olib tashlandingiz» without buttons for a removed assignee, «Tizim topshirig'i · …» / «✅ Yopildi · Tizim» for a system task), then title, Bergan / Muddat / Bog'liq / Kichik qadamlar / «Rasm bilan tasdiqlanadi»; a closed task keeps only headline, title, the «who» line and the time. Who did what comes from `TgTaskView.history` (`loadTaskView`: the last ≤ 10 `STATUS` / `RETURN` / `CANCELLED` / `AUTO_CLOSED` events with actor, time and reason). The **notice** `renderTaskMessage` (COMMENT, REMINDER, OVERDUE): headline + title + the notice's line + the same rows, HTML-escaped. Buttons come from the task's state and the reader: assignee «Boshladim» (NEW) and «Bajardim» (on a `requiresPhoto` task without a fresh photo the press sends the «Rasmni shu xabarga javob qilib yuboring» force-reply prompt, recorded as `PHOTO_PROMPT`; with a fresh photo it moves the task as usual), author «Qabul qilish» / «Qaytarish» in review, «Qadamlar», and «Ochish» — a **`web_app` button** (`Markup.button.webApp`, opens inside Telegram without a password) to the reader's staff Mini App (`taskOpenUrl`, `task-telegram-view.ts`: portal from `staffPortalFor`, host from `TELEGRAM_MINI_APP_URL` via `staffMiniAppUrl`, page `/tg?next=/tasks?task=<id>`; the client's `staffCabinetPath` admits `/tasks?task=<uuid>` and nothing else new); unset → no «Ochish». A live card of an open task and the reminder carry the state buttons; a closed task's card, comment and overdue carry «Ochish» alone. A system task gets «Ochish» only. Every message the bot sends about a task is a `TaskTelegramMessage` row (`chatId`, `messageId` → `taskId`, `userId`, `purpose` `CARD` | `NOTICE` | `RETURN_PROMPT` | `PHOTO_PROMPT`); the one sender is `TaskTelegramSender` (`send` for notices and first cards, `record` for messages the handler sent itself).
  - **Buttons** (`TaskTelegramHandler`): registered in `onApplicationBootstrap` through `TelegramService.useBeforeScenes(...)`, a slot composed per update right BEFORE the scene stage (after the session, the `/start` reset and the channel gate). So a press, or a reply to a task message, works while an old bot flow (registration, password reset) is open, and the handler calls `next()` for everything else. Callback data is `tk:<action>:<taskId>`, `tk:step:<stepId>`, `tk:list` (≤ 64 bytes). The chat's one live staff account (`staffOfChat`; none → «Bu topshiriq sizda emas», two → refused) acts through `TasksService` with `via: 'TELEGRAM'` — the website's policy and transitions, nothing extra. A state button («Boshladim», «Bajardim», «Qabul qilish», «Qadamlar», «Orqaga», a step tick) edits the same message, **redrawn as the presser's live card** (`drawCard` → `renderLiveCard`; there is no kept headline any more — the state names the headline); the other people's cards are redrawn by the listener (see «Live cards»); a stale press answers the service's reason and redraws the task as it is now; a task the person can no longer see loses its buttons. Two presses send a NEW message instead: «Qaytarish» (a force-reply prompt that names the task and replies to the pressed message) and a number in «Topshiriqlarim» (that task's live card, recorded as a `CARD` row, so it is edited from then on; the list stays).
  - **Live cards** (`TaskTelegramLive.refresh(taskId)`, `task-telegram-live.ts`, ADR-0089): every `CARD` row of the task is edited in place (`editMessageText`), each rendered for its own `userId`. `TaskTelegramListener.handle` calls it FIRST, before any notice, on ASSIGNED, UNASSIGNED, STATUS_CHANGED, REVIEW_REQUESTED, REVIEWED, CANCELLED, REASSIGNED, DUE_CHANGED and AUTO_CLOSED (`REFRESH_EVENTS`; a COMMENT changes no card). Edits make no sound, so they **ignore the night quiet** — only a NEW first card waits for 08:00 through the outbox. Edit failures: «message is not modified» is fine; «message to edit not found» / «message can't be edited» (the person deleted it) or a permanent failure (bot blocked, chat gone) **deletes the row**, so the next status notice sends a fresh card; a 429 waits `retry_after` once (≤ 30 s) and a second 429 stops this refresh (the later cards would hit it too — the next event redraws them); anything else is logged and the next card is tried. `refresh` never throws. Known race, accepted: two events in the same instant can both see no card and send two; the next refresh keeps both right. Messages sent before the deploy are `NOTICE` rows and are never edited — the first status change after the deploy sends each person one `CARD`.
  - **Replies:** a text reply to a `NOTICE` or `CARD` message is a comment, to a `RETURN_PROMPT` the return reason (at most `REVIEW_REASON_MAX` = 1000 characters, checked in `TasksService.review` because the bot skips the DTO). A photo (largest size), document, video or voice replying to a `NOTICE`, `CARD` or `PHOTO_PROMPT` is downloaded (`getFileLink` + `downloadFile`; over 20 MB or a voice over 180 s refused before downloading, stickers / music / round videos answered `UNSUPPORTED_MEDIA`) and stored through `TaskFilesService.upload` (`via` TELEGRAM, so the same type sniffing and limits as the website); a caption becomes a comment; an IMAGE answering `PHOTO_PROMPT` then sends the task to review (a non-image keeps asking, `PHOTO_STILL_NEEDED`). A text reply to `PHOTO_PROMPT` repeats the prompt; a file replying to `RETURN_PROMPT` is refused (the reason is text). The reply side lives in `telegram/task-telegram-replies.ts` (`TaskTelegramReplies`), the attachment picker in `task-telegram-media.ts`, identity in `task-telegram-actor.ts`. A message that is not a reply to a message recorded for THIS chat, or whose text starts with a bot command, goes on to the bot's own flows (`next()`).
  - **«📋 Topshiriqlarim»:** the person's open ASSIGNEE tasks except «Dars bo'ldimi?», due-date order (undated last), grouped «Muddati o'tgan / Bugun / Keyinroq», the first 10 as number buttons (the rest is counted: «Yana N ta topshiriq saytda.»); a number sends the task card as a new message. A staff menu sent before the deploy has no such row until the person sends `/start`. «Qadamlar» turns the message into the step list (✓ toggles through `TasksService.updateStep`, «Orqaga» goes back).
- **Lesson task** (`lesson-task.ts`): «Dars bo'ldimi?» (ADR-0054) is a `Task` of kind `LESSON_QUESTION` with `authorId: null` and `sourceKey` `unmarked:<groupId>:<date>`, created by `createLessonTask` straight in the question's transaction. `closeLessonTask` closes it (`AUTO_CLOSED`) on an answer, and `closeTasksOfDeletedGroup` (`unmarked-lessons/unmarked-lesson-transitions.ts`) calls it when the group is deleted. `UnmarkedLesson.taskId` points at the task (`taskCommentId` stays until the cleanup PR). The rest of ADR-0054 («Taking the task», answers) is unchanged.
- **Automatic tasks** (ADR-0083; spec §7). Every rule is in `task-auto-rules.ts` (`AUTO_RULES`: priority, entity type, `sourceKey` prefix, `closesOn`; texts, `systemDueAt`, the assignee ladder). Writers: `task-auto-write.ts` — `createSystemTask` (author Tizim, one OPEN task per `sourceKey` via `task_open_source_unique`, a concurrent duplicate returns null; time rows through `scheduleTaskOutbox`) and `autoCloseTasks` (DONE + `AUTO_CLOSED` with `meta.reason`, unsent outbox rows dropped; never touches `MANUAL`, `LESSON_QUESTION` or `JOIN_REQUEST`). Creators emit the ordinary `task.assigned` (`actorId: null`, `created: true`), so the bell, push, SSE and Telegram need nothing special.
  - `BROKEN_PROMISE` — `TaskAutoService.onPromisesBroken` on `payment-promise.overdue`: ONE task per student (the latest promise speaks for them) who still owes `MIN_ALERT_DEBT` and has no open «Buzilgan va'da» task, `sourceKey` `promise:<id>`, entity Student, branch ladder (`autoTaskAssigneeIds`), due today 18:00. The hourly run below retries it for the promises broken today.
  - `CALLBACK` — `TaskAutoService.onCallLogged` on `call-log.created` (emitted by `CallLogsService.create` after its writes) when `followUpAt` is set: `calllog:<id>`, the caller only (while active), due the follow-up day 18:00. Its Telegram notice waits for 08:00 of that day (`telegramNotBefore`, held as a `NOTICE` outbox row); the bell gets it at once.
  - `UNCALLED_LEAD` — `TaskAutoLeadsService`, `@Cron('0 0 8-18 * * 1-6')` Tashkent: NEW, uncalled, live leads older than 24 h and created at or after `Company.uncalledLeadTasksFrom` (the migration's moment — older leads never get one), `lead:<id>`, the lead's branch ladder (no branch → the company's administrators, then directors, then CEOs), due today 18:00; at most 200 a company a run. A lead is tasked once, ever (an un-marked call does not open a second task). The same run first closes the sweeps that events do not reach: tasks whose lead no longer waits (`LEAD_GONE` — a section archived with its leads sends no event) and the callback / broken-promise tasks of an archived student (`STUDENT_GONE`) and the «Buzilgan va'da» task of a student who no longer owes `MIN_ALERT_DEBT` (`DEBT_CLEARED`: a cancelled-lesson refund, a write-off or a centre top-up clear a debt with no `payment.received`); then it retries today's «Buzilgan va'da» tasks the 09:00 event failed to make (from 10:00; per student: a task open, created today, or closed or cancelled today counts as handled). Each step of each company fails alone.
  - **Due:** 18:00 Tashkent, off Sundays and the branch's holidays; a time already past moves to the next working day 18:00 (`systemDueAt`) — a task is never born overdue.
  - **Closing** is done by `TaskAutoCloseListener` (events) and the hourly sweep of `TaskAutoLeadsService` (`LEAD_GONE`, `STUDENT_GONE`, `DEBT_CLEARED`). The listener: `payment.received` closes the student's `CALLBACK` always but a `BROKEN_PROMISE` only when the debt is gone (`paymentClosesBrokenPromise`: balance above −`MIN_ALERT_DEBT`, 1 000 so'm; a partial payment, or an unknown balance, keeps it open); `call-log.created` closes both, whatever the result, except the callback the same call asked for; `lead.called` (`LeadsService.markCalled`, first mark only) and `entity.status.changed` for a Lead close `UNCALLED_LEAD`. Each close emits `task.auto-closed { task, reason }`: the resolver closes the task's bell rows, `TaskNotifyListener` refreshes open pages over SSE; no notice is written.
  - The first assignee to start one takes it (`claimSystemTask`, as «Dars bo'ldimi?»). Telegram headline: «🤖 Tizim topshirig'i» (priority on the ⏰ line), «📂 Ochish» button only. `GET /tasks/:id` carries `source` (`task-source.ts`): the student (name, phone, debt, last call) with the promise or the call that asked for the callback and the reason the call dialog pre-fills, or the lead (name, phone, source, created); an archived student or lead gives none.
  - **21:00 report:** one line above «🚩 Diqqat» — `📋 Topshiriqlar: ochiq N, muddati o'tgan M. Eng ko'p kechikkan: <ism> — K` (`task-report-figures.ts` + `telegram-groups/utils/task-line.util.ts`), the report's branch scope, none when M = 0, not a flag (it never changes the day's colour), a failing query drops the line only.
- **Join request task** (`join-request-task.ts`, ADR-0080): kind `JOIN_REQUEST`, `sourceKey` `join:<requestId>`, the same assignee ladder and claim rule as «Dars bo'ldimi?», due the next working day 10:00. Unlike the lesson question it goes to Telegram (a card with «Ochish» only — a system task has no state buttons). It closes only with its request (`closeJoinRequestTask`, `AUTO_CLOSED` with `JOIN_APPROVED | JOIN_REJECTED | JOIN_EXPIRED | JOIN_REPLACED`); `JOIN_REQUEST_CLOSED` closes its bell rows (`NotificationResolverService.onJoinRequestClosed`).
- **Recurring tasks** (ADR-0084; spec §3.6). A rule is a `TaskRecurrence` row (schedule `freq` DAILY/WEEKLY/MONTHLY, `weekdays` 1–6, `dayOfMonth` 1–28, `dueTime` "HH:mm" 08:00–22:00; template title/description/priority/assignees/watchers/steps/link/`separateCopies`; `authorId`, `branchId`, `active`, `nextRunDate` `@db.Date`); its tasks are MANUAL with `Task.recurrenceId` (FK `ON DELETE SET NULL`). Date math is pure in `task-recurrence-rules.ts` (`nextRunDateAfter` skips Sundays and the branch's holidays; days pushed onto one working day make one run). `POST /tasks` with `recurrence` goes to `TaskRecurrencesService.create` (no `dueAt` allowed, active staff only → else 400): the same checks and writer as a one-off create (`TasksService.prepareCreate`, `createCopiesTx`), the rule and its FIRST task in one transaction, due on the first run day whose time is still ahead. `TaskRecurrenceRunner` (`@Cron('0 0 7 * * *')` Tashkent, 500 rules a run): rules with `nextRunDate <= today` (a missed morning is made up once; nothing on a Sunday/holiday); the author's rights re-checked (author deactivated or link gone → the rule stops itself), people re-filtered (`reachablePeople`: active staff on the author's ladder); then ONE transaction whose first write is `updateMany({ where: { id, active, nextRunDate: <read> } })` → `count 0` means another instance has it (the idempotency key); while a task of the rule is open no new one (per assignee for separate copies, `runGroups`) but the date still moves; nobody left → skipped, date moves; `task.assigned` after the commit with `actorId = author`. Routes: `GET /tasks/recurrences` (CEO company, Branch Director own + branches, others own), `PATCH /tasks/recurrences/:id` (`schedule`, `active`, `assigneeIds` only; a new schedule or a real resume starts tomorrow, assignees alone keep `nextRunDate`), `DELETE /tasks/recurrences/:id` (the rule goes, its tasks stay); manage = author / Branch Director of the rule's branch / CEO, anyone else 404. Cards carry `recurrence` (badge, null once stopped); `GET /tasks/:id` carries `recurrence` (the rule's view with `canManage`; assignees named only for the author and managers).
- **Files** (ADR-0086; spec §3.5, §9.4). `TaskFile` rows (`key`, `name`, `size`, `mime`, `kind` IMAGE / DOCUMENT / VOICE, `durationSec`, `uploadedById`, `via`, `deletedAt`, `deletedById`, `purgedAt`) live in the PRIVATE bucket `R2_TASKS_BUCKET_NAME` (`TaskFileStorage`; absent → `GET /tasks/config` `filesEnabled: false`, upload and delete answer 503 «Fayllar hozircha o'chiq»). Upload goes THROUGH the server (`POST /tasks/:id/files`, multipart `file` + `name` + `durationSec`, `TaskFileUploadInterceptor`: memory storage, 20 MB, one file, two text fields) — not a presigned PUT, so there is no CORS rule and no unconfirmed row: the type is read from the BYTES (`task-file-rules.ts`: jpg / png / webp → IMAGE; pdf, docx, xlsx, mp4 and mov video → DOCUMENT; ogg / webm / m4a audio → VOICE, at most 180 s declared and 2 MB), the name's extension is replaced by the real one, the object is `PutObject`ed under `tasks/<companyId>/<taskId>/<uuid><ext>`, then the row and its `FILE` / `VOICE` event commit together (a failed commit deletes the object). Known limits: the 20 MB body is buffered before the 503 / 403 checks in `TaskFilesService.upload` (the route is `@AnyStaff()`, so only staff can make the server hold it), and an audio-only mp4 with an `isom` / `mp42` brand (Android recorders) is typed as video, not voice. `GET /tasks/:id/files` re-checks access like the task and mints 10-minute presigned GETs (`url` inline, `downloadUrl` attachment), plus `hasFreshPhoto` and `enabled`. **Upload: the assignee or a manager** (`access.canWork || access.canManage`; a watcher gets 403 «Fayl qo'shish uchun ijrochi yoki beruvchi bo'lish kerak»); view and download: anyone who can view the task. **Delete: the uploader or `canManage`** → soft (`deletedAt`, a `FILE_DELETED` row «faylni o'chirdi»); the 03:00 Tashkent `TaskFilesService.purge` deletes the objects 30 days later and stamps `purgedAt` (rows stay). A new file is announced as `task.commented` with `attachment: 'FILE' | 'VOICE'` (bell «Yangi fayl» / «Ovozli xabar» to the author, assignees and watchers except the actor; Telegram, headline «Yangi fayl» / «Yangi ovozli xabar», to the author and assignees only, never watchers). Never log a presigned URL or a Telegram file link.
- **Visibility.** `GET /tasks?view=my|created|all`: `my` = assigned to me, `created` = written by me, `all` = `visibilityWhere` for EVERY role (CEO everything, a Branch Director their branches' tasks plus their own, anyone else the tasks they wrote or take part in). The entity panels use `view=all&entityType&entityId`. `GET /tasks/workload` is for managers only.
- **Departed staff** (`task-user-lifecycle.listener.ts`, `USER_DEACTIVATED_EVENT`): when the last assignee leaves, a manual task goes back to its author while the author is active, otherwise to the branch's lesson-question ladder (`lessonTaskAssigneeIds`); every system task goes to `autoTaskAssigneeIds` (since A10 a branch-less one — `LESSON_QUESTION` and `JOIN_REQUEST` included — goes to the company-wide ladder); the leaver's unsent outbox rows go.
- **Comments no longer carry tasks.** `POST /comments` takes `{ entityType, entityId, content }` only (`forbidNonWhitelisted` → 400 for `isTask` / `assigneeIds` / `dueDate` / `priority`); lists hide `isTask = true` rows; `my-tasks`, `created-tasks`, `assignee-status` and `TaskReminderService` are gone. `Comment.isTask`, `Comment.migratedTaskId` and `CommentAssignee` stay for the migration only. `PATCH /comments/:id` (author or CEO) and `DELETE` (CEO) are as before; comment creation is still audited through `EntityHistoryService`.
- **Migration** (`scripts/migrate-comment-tasks.ts`, with `scripts/lib/comment-task-apply.ts`): dry run by default (read-only connection, checked), `--apply` writes one transaction per comment and stamps `Comment.migratedTaskId`, so a rerun skips finished rows. Run it ONCE right after the deploy, outside working hours: `railway run --service caring-courage --environment production npx ts-node --transpile-only scripts/migrate-comment-tasks.ts [--apply]`. Until it has run, /tasks is empty.

### Student chat (`src/chat/`, spec 2026-10-25)

Student-to-student private chat, behind the company switch `chat.enabled` (default off): while it is off every `/chat/*` student route except `GET /chat/access` (which reports `enabled`) is refused with 403 CHAT_OFF_MESSAGE by `ChatEnabledGuard`; the CEO routes under `/chat/admin/*` are not gated.

- **One access rule.** `ChatAccessService.forStudent` is the only place that decides whether a student may write: `balance >= 0`, gender and birth date set, the card's phone proved (`isPhoneVerified` — NOT `missingOnboardingSteps`, which only asks for the phone while `STUDENT_PHONE_VERIFICATION_ENABLED` is on), not banned, and the last 7 Tashkent days (today + 6) hold at least `chat.weekMinutesActive` active and `chat.weekMinutesRadio` radio minutes in `StudentAppSession`. The pure rules and the Uzbek texts are `chat/access/chat-access.rules.ts`; clients print the texts the API returns and compute nothing.
- **Reading is never closed.** A locked student reads everything and receives messages; only writing (and opening a new conversation) is refused.
- **Send checks run in this order:** access → membership (404 for a conversation that is not yours) → block → the peer's `whoCanWrite` → 30/minute rate limit. The peer's block and the peer's privacy refusal share one message (RESTRICTED_MESSAGE) so being blocked is never revealed; a block the caller placed gets its own message.
- Opening a conversation by raw student id requires the peer's `whoCanFind` to allow it or a shared group; an exact `@username` always works. New conversations are limited to 10 per minute.
- `GET /chat/me` also returns `blockedIds` (students I blocked), so the client can offer unblock after a reload.
- Name search finds students who are studying (`activeStudentWhere()`); an exact `@username` finds anyone.
- **Another student sees a name, `@username` and (if allowed) a photo — never a phone, a debt or a balance.** `PeerSummary` is the only shape that leaves.
- **The conversation list is batched** (≤ 6 queries whatever the page size): `ChatConversationsService.list` takes cards from `ChatPeopleService.peerSummaries` (the single implementation of the photo-privacy rule, `peerSummary` calls it too), the last non-deleted message with one `DISTINCT ON` raw query and the unread counts with one raw query that joins `ChatReadState`; never go back to per-conversation queries.
- **Live delivery** goes through `NotificationsGateway.sendToUser(student.userId, …)` as `chat.message` / `chat.read`; the existing stream carries them.
- **Media (Plan B).** Images (jpg/png/webp ≤ 5 MB) and voice (ogg/webm/m4a ≤ 120 s, ≤ 2 MB) go through `POST /chat/conversations/:id/files` into the private bucket `R2_CHAT_BUCKET_NAME` (off without it → 503); the type is read from the bytes (`chat/media/chat-file-rules.ts` reuses `checkTaskFile`). A message exposes `file { mime, sizeBytes, durationSec, purged }`, never the key; `GET /chat/messages/:id/file` returns a 10-minute signed link to a member (410 once purged). Text and media share `prepareSend` (every check) and `commitMessage`.
- **Purge.** `ChatMediaPurgeService` (03:00 Tashkent) removes the file of a message its sender deleted, or of a conversation in which neither student had an app session for 30 days, and stamps `mediaPurgedAt`. Text and report snapshots stay.
- **Reports and ban.** `POST /chat/report` (any member, locked or banned too) keeps a TEXT copy of the last 50 messages (deleted ones flagged); one OPEN report per reporter and reported. The CEO reviews on `/chat/admin/reports*` (`@CeoOnly()`), may ban (`Student.chatBannedAt`, Student history, live `chat.access` event) and unban; a banned student still reads and reports.
- **Notifications.** After the commit, `ChatNotifyService` writes one unread `CHAT_MESSAGE` bell row per conversation (group `system`, folded by `groupKey = CHAT_MESSAGE:<conversationId>`, marked read by `markRead`), pushes it and sends ONE Telegram notice per conversation until the recipient reads it (ADR-0129: instant, `web_app` button to the Mini App chat, nothing 22:00–08:00, held notices go at 08:00; a Telegram chat the bot marked disconnected is skipped, ADR-0117 precedent; the 08:00 run re-reads the recipient and the `chat.enabled` switch). Content never appears in any notice. It never throws.
- Clients arrive with Plan C.

### Notifications (4 channels)

- `NotificationsModule` (`src/notifications/`) — notification system. Decision record: ADR-0076 (`docs/adr/0076-qongiroqcha-kutilayotgan-xabar-ozi-yopiladi.md`); design: `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-07-topshiriqlar-design.md` §8.
- **Notification** table: per-user rows (`userId`, `type`, `title`, `message`, `isRead`) plus the bell's state: `actionRequired` (waits for the recipient), `resolvedAt` (the job it asked for got done; `null` = still open), `groupKey` (rows the client folds into one line). **PushSubscription**: browser push subscriptions.
- **One writer.** Every row goes through `NotificationsService.create`, which stamps `actionRequired` and `groupKey` with `notificationKind` (`notification-kind.ts`, pure, tested) and returns the row with its `group` (`NotificationView`, also what SSE carries). The action types (`ACTION_TYPES`) are `ATTENDANCE_ADMIN_ALERT`, `ATTENDANCE_TEACHER_WARNING`, `ATTENDANCE_MISSING_TEACHER`, `ATTENDANCE_MISSING_ADMIN`, `LESSON_STARTED`, `TASK_ASSIGNED`, `TASK_REMINDER`, `TASK_REVIEW`, `PAYMENT_PROMISE_OVERDUE`; everything else is information. `task-notify-plan.ts` overrides it per notice through `create({ actionRequired })` (a watcher's «Kuzatuvchi qilindingiz» is information, «Topshiriq qaytarildi» waits). `groupKey = <TYPE>:<YYYY-MM-DD>` (Tashkent) for the five lesson alerts (`LESSON_ALERT_TYPES`) only — they are sent on the lesson's own day, so the key carries the lesson date; per type, so «30 daqiqa qoldi» and «dars tugadi» never fold together. `NOTIFICATION_GROUP` is a `Record` over the enum: a new `NotificationType` does not compile until it is placed in `task` / `attendance` / `payment` / `system`.
- **The badge counts only what waits:** `actionRequired && resolvedAt == null && !isRead` (`GET /notifications/unread-count`). Reading a row clears it from the badge; only the job done clears it from «Kutilmoqda» (`filter=pending` = `actionRequired && resolvedAt == null`, read or not).
- **Alerts close themselves.** `NotificationResolverService` (`notification-resolver.service.ts`) hears events that are emitted after their transaction commits, stamps `resolvedAt` on the matching OPEN action rows of every recipient (information rows are never touched, every write carries the event's `companyId`) and sends `{ type: 'notification.resolved', ids, resolvedAt }` over SSE to each owner, only when rows really changed. A new action type needs its closing event here: only the lesson alerts have a backstop (the 03:00 rule at the foot of the table), and a row with neither stays in «Kutilmoqda» for ever — the one-time cleanup ran once, at the deploy. `event-wiring.spec.ts` keeps every event it listens to emitted.

  | Event | Closes |
  | --- | --- |
  | `attendance.completed`, `attendance.student.recorded` (a QR-only register emits only this), `lesson-cancellation.created`, `lesson-reschedule.created` (its `originalDate`) | that group's five lesson alerts of that day |
  | `unmarked-lesson.held` / `unmarked-lesson.not-held` / `unmarked-lesson.closed` (the question closed unanswered: its make-up day lost its lesson, or its group was deleted) | the same, plus the notices of the closed «Dars bo'ldimi?» task |
  | `group.deleted` (`groups/group-events.ts`) | every lesson alert of that group, whatever the day |
  | `task.status.changed`, `task.cancelled`, `task.reviewed` that leave the task DONE/CANCELLED | every notice of the task |
  | `task.status.changed` on an open non-`MANUAL` task (the first assignee took a system task) | the notices of the administrators who lost it (not the remaining participants, not the author) |
  | `task.reviewed` (either answer) | the task's `TASK_REVIEW` rows |
  | `task.unassigned` | the removed users' rows of that task |
  | `task.auto-closed` (an automatic task closed by its source, ADR-0083) | every notice of the task |
  | `payment.received` with `studentBalance >= 0` | an old per-student `PAYMENT_PROMISE_OVERDUE` row (`relatedEntityType 'Student'`; the same test as `settleKeptPromises`) |
  | `payment.received`, any balance | the 09:00 list (`relatedEntityType 'BrokenPromises'`, ADR-0076) the student is on, once nobody on it still owes. A list = the open rows of one company, one branch key (`relatedEntityId`: the branch id, or 'all' = promises with no branch) and one Tashkent day of `createdAt`; its students = the BROKEN promises of that branch key whose `reminderFiredAt` falls on that day; a student owes at `Student.balance <= -MIN_ALERT_DEBT` (`overdue-digest.ts`; a smaller debt was never listed). Balances are read after the commit, not from the event. All recipients' rows close together |
  | `payment-promise.overdue` (the cron's per-branch event) | the same branch's older open lists: rows created before today's Tashkent start, never the rows of the run that is creating them (the listener and the resolver hear the same event, so order does not matter) |
  | every night at 03:00 Asia/Tashkent (`@Cron`, `closePastLessonAlerts`, all companies) | every open lesson alert (`LESSON_ALERT_TYPES`, `relatedEntityType 'Group'`) whose lesson day — the date in its `groupKey` — is before today (Tashkent) and for whose group and day no PENDING «Dars bo'ldimi?» question of a live (not deleted) group waits. Today stays: its question may still open tonight. A PENDING question keeps the alerts: the administrator still owes the answer, and giving it closes them through the events above. The one SQL definition is `pastLessonAlerts` (`past-lesson-alerts.ts`); step 2 of the cleanup script runs the same fragment. The ids go through `resolve`, per company in chunks of 500, so the owners' bells are told (only for rows that really changed). The sweep is skipped with `CRONS_ENABLED=false` like every cron |

  Every lesson row in the table also edits its Telegram copy with the outcome the handler names (`TAKEN`, `CANCELLED`, `MOVED`, `HELD`, `NOT_HELD`, `CLOSED`, `GROUP_DELETED`; the 03:00 sweep `EXPIRED`) — see channel 4 below (ADR-0090). **The first event to close the row wins** (the edit runs only on a write with `count > 0`; a later event finds the row closed and does nothing), so `HELD`, `NOT_HELD` and `CLOSED` are fallback headlines in practice: «Bo'ldi» (`saveLate`) emits `attendance.student.recorded` before `UNMARKED_LESSON_HELD`, so the administrator reads «✅ Davomat olindi»; «Bo'lmadi» writes a cancellation or a move first, so «❌ Dars bekor qilindi» / «🔀 Dars ko'chirildi»; a deleted group's `GROUP_DELETED` arrives before its question closes, so «🗑 Guruh o'chirildi». The three fallbacks show only when that earlier event closed nothing (e.g. «Bo'ldi» on a day with an empty roster).

  Known gaps — what nothing closes (the one-time cleanup ran once, at the deploy; the 03:00 rule covers only lesson alerts):
  - A debt cleared without a payment (write-off, centre top-up) never turns a BROKEN promise KEPT and sends no `payment.received`, so its 09:00 list stays open until the branch's next list replaces it — and for ever when that branch breaks no promise again.
  - An old per-student overdue row (`relatedEntityType 'Student'`, written before the deploy) that was under 7 days old at the cleanup, for a student who still owed, stays until a payment clears that student's debt.
  - A task notice whose task never closes (a task nobody finishes) waits as long as the task does: a returned «Topshiriq qaytarildi» notice, an assignee removed before the deploy, a system task lost to a claim before the deploy. They all close with the task.
  - A lesson alert is closed by the 03:00 rule only on the day after its lesson. The paths that used to strand it — a group that left ACTIVE on its lesson day after the start or −30 alerts (COMPLETED on the final day, CANCELLED, PAUSED, a closed branch; the sweep asks ACTIVE groups only), a holiday entered on the day, the check-then-act race at the lesson's start — wait at most until the next 03:00. A day with a PENDING question waits for its answer.

- **No alert for a lesson nobody can act on.** `AttendanceReminderService.tick` drops a group whose lesson today is cancelled or moved away (`groupsStillMeeting`, the sweep's own `lessonsOn` rule, two queries per tick), so none of the three time-triggered alerts is born for it; `LESSON_STARTED` is also not sent when the lesson already has a register (one taken ahead of the lesson start). A «Topshiriq qaytarildi» notice stays waiting after the assignee resubmits for review, until the task closes.
- **Endpoints** (all SELF in the route manifest, `common/auth/branch-route-policy.ts` — keyed on the caller, no branch question):
  - `GET /api/notifications?filter=pending|all&type=task|attendance|payment|system&q=&cursor=&pageSize=` → `{ data, nextCursor }`, newest first (`createdAt desc, id desc`), keyset cursor from `tasks/task-cursor.ts` (`cursorWhere`, `encodeCursor`, `decodeCursor`; a bad cursor is 400); `filter` defaults to `all`, `pageSize` 1–50 (default 20), `q` searches title and message (max 100 chars); every row carries `group`. `page` (`NotificationQueryDto`) is accepted and ignored — the pre-phase-5 bell sends `page=1&pageSize=20` and keeps working between the server and the Vercel deploys.
  - `GET /api/notifications/unread-count` → `{ count }` — the badge rule above
  - `GET /api/notifications/counts` → `{ pending, all, groups }` — the page's left list (`NotificationsService.getCounts`)
  - `PATCH /api/notifications/:id/read`, `PATCH /api/notifications/read-all`
  - `GET /api/notifications/stream` — SSE (fetch-based, JWT header): `notification` (a new row), `notification.resolved`, `task.updated`
  - `POST /api/notifications/push/subscribe`, `DELETE /api/notifications/push/unsubscribe`, `POST|DELETE /api/notifications/devices` (Expo token), `GET /api/notifications/vapid-public-key` (public)
- **4 delivery channels:**
  1. **DB** — every notification is persisted
  2. **SSE** (`notifications.gateway.ts`): userId → Response mapping, 30 s heartbeat
  3. **Web Push** — works even when the browser is closed, via `web-push` and VAPID keys
  4. **Telegram** — queued for the 20:00 digest (see "Telegram digest" below) and delivered only if the recipient has a `telegramChatId` at send time; `payment-promise.overdue` (09:00) and `salary.paid` (the staff salary message, ADR-0082 — Telegram only, no bell row) are the listener events still sent instantly. Task notices are not here: `src/tasks/telegram/` sends them itself (Tasks → Telegram, ADR-0078). Closing a bell row does not touch Telegram — except a lesson alert (ADR-0090): `AttendanceReminderService.deliver` stores the sent message's chat and id on the row (`Notification.telegramChatId` / `telegramMessageId`), and `NotificationResolverService.resolve(where, outcome)` edits that message when the row closes, with an outcome headline (`resolvedLessonAlertText`, `lesson-alert-telegram.ts`: `✅ Davomat olindi · HH:mm`, `❌ Dars bekor qilindi`, `🔀 Dars ko'chirildi`, `✅ «Bo'ldi» deb belgilandi`, `❌ «Bo'lmadi» deb belgilandi`, `☑️ Savol yopildi`, `🗑 Guruh o'chirildi`, `☑️ Yopildi`), the lesson details in a blockquote and the old title; the instruction and the link are dropped. Edits are silent and sequential; «message is not modified» is ignored, any other failure is logged and `resolve` still returns its count. Only the lesson handlers pass an outcome, so task and payment rows are never edited; copies sent before the deploy have no id and stay as they are.
